import { edgeErrorResponse } from "../_shared/edgeRequest.ts";
import {
  asObjectRecord,
  asString,
} from "../_shared/payloadNormalization.ts";

/** Billing-relevant fields from task_types, joined via FK */
interface TaskTypeConfig {
  id: string;
  billing_type: string;
  base_cost_per_second: number;
  unit_cost: number;
  cost_factors: CostFactors | null;
  is_active: boolean;
}

/** Shape returned by tasks.select('..., projects(user_id), task_types!(...)') with joined project + task type */
interface TaskWithProject {
  id: string;
  task_type: string;
  params: TaskCostParams;
  status: string;
  generation_started_at: string | null;
  generation_processed_at: string | null;
  project_id: string;
  projects: { user_id: string };
  task_type_config: TaskTypeConfig | null;
}

interface TaskParseFailure {
  errorCode: "task_shape_invalid";
  message: string;
  details: Record<string, unknown>;
}

type TaskParseResult =
  | { ok: true; task: TaskWithProject }
  | { ok: false; failure: TaskParseFailure };

function asNumber(value: unknown): number | undefined {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
}

export function parseTaskCostParams(value: unknown): TaskCostParams {
  const record = asObjectRecord(value) ?? {};
  const parsed: TaskCostParams = {};

  const resolution = asString(record.resolution);
  if (resolution) {
    parsed.resolution = resolution;
  }

  const frameCount = asNumber(record.frame_count);
  if (frameCount !== undefined) {
    parsed.frame_count = frameCount;
  }

  const modelType = asString(record.model_type);
  if (modelType) {
    parsed.model_type = modelType;
  }

  const result = asObjectRecord(record.result);
  if (result) {
    parsed.result = result as VideoEnhanceResult;
  }

  for (const [key, rawValue] of Object.entries(record)) {
    // Never re-inject known billing keys unless they pass explicit normalization.
    if (key === 'resolution' || key === 'frame_count' || key === 'model_type' || key === 'result') {
      continue;
    }
    parsed[key] = rawValue;
  }

  return parsed;
}

export function parseTaskWithProject(value: unknown): TaskParseResult {
  const task = asObjectRecord(value);
  if (!task) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      failure: {
        errorCode: "task_shape_invalid",
        message: "Task payload is not an object",
        details: { received_type: typeof value },
      },
    };
  }

  const id = asString(task.id);
  const taskType = asString(task.task_type);
  const projectId = asString(task.project_id);
  if (!id || !taskType || !projectId) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      failure: {
        errorCode: "task_shape_invalid",
        message: "Task payload missing required fields: id, task_type, or project_id",
        details: {
          has_id: !!id,
          has_task_type: !!taskType,
          has_project_id: !!projectId,
        },
      },
    };
  }

  const projectsRaw = task.projects;
  const projectRecord = asObjectRecord(projectsRaw)
    ?? (Array.isArray(projectsRaw) ? asObjectRecord(projectsRaw[0]) : null);
  const userId = asString(projectRecord?.user_id);
  if (!userId) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      failure: {
        errorCode: "task_shape_invalid",
        message: "Task payload missing projects.user_id",
        details: {
          projects_type: Array.isArray(projectsRaw) ? "array" : typeof projectsRaw,
          has_projects_user_id: false,
        },
      },
    };
  }

  const params = parseTaskCostParams(task.params);
  const status = asString(task.status) ?? "unknown";

  // Extract task_types FK join (PostgREST returns object or null)
  let taskTypeConfig: TaskTypeConfig | null = null;
  const ttRaw = asObjectRecord(task.task_types);
  if (ttRaw) {
    const ttId = asString(ttRaw.id);
    const billingType = asString(ttRaw.billing_type);
    if (ttId && billingType) {
      taskTypeConfig = {
        id: ttId,
        billing_type: billingType,
        base_cost_per_second: typeof ttRaw.base_cost_per_second === 'number' ? ttRaw.base_cost_per_second : 0,
        unit_cost: typeof ttRaw.unit_cost === 'number' ? ttRaw.unit_cost : 0,
        cost_factors: asObjectRecord(ttRaw.cost_factors) as CostFactors | null,
        is_active: ttRaw.is_active === true,
      };
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
```

// deno-lint-ignore-file
import { jsonResponse } from "../_shared/http.ts";
import {
  enforceRateLimit,
  RATE_LIMITS,
} from "../_shared/rateLimit.ts";
import { bootstrapEdgeHandler, NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS } from "../_shared/edgeHandler.ts";
import { resolveTaskStorageActor } from "../_shared/taskActorPolicy.ts";

// Import from refactored modules
import { parseCompleteTaskRequest, validateStoragePathSecurity } from './request.ts';
import { handleStorageOperations, getStoragePublicUrl, cleanupFile } from './storage.ts';
import { setThumbnailInParams } from './params.ts';
import { createGenerationFromTask } from './generation.ts';
import { checkOrchestratorCompletion } from './orchestrator.ts';
import { validateAndCleanupShotId } from './shotValidation.ts';
import { triggerCostCalculationIfNotSubTask } from './billing.ts';
import {
  completeTaskErrorResponse,
  fetchTaskContext,
  markTaskFailed,
  persistCompletionFollowUpIssues,
  type CompletionFollowUpIssue,
} from './completionHelpers.ts';

// Provide a loose Deno type for local tooling
declare const Deno: { env: { get: (key: string) => string | undefined } };

export async function completeTaskHandler(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler(req, {
    functionName: "complete-task",
    logPrefix: "[COMPLETE-TASK]",
    parseBody: "none",
    auth: {
      required: true,
      options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
    },
    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,
  });
  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
    return bootstrap.response;
  }

  const { supabaseAdmin, logger, auth } = bootstrap.value;
  if (!auth || (!auth.userId && !auth.isServiceRole)) {
    logger.error("Authentication failed");
    await logger.flush();
    return completeTaskErrorResponse("Authentication failed", 401);
  }

  // 1) Parse and validate request
  const parseResult = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(req);
  if (!parseResult.success) {
    return parseResult.response;
  }
  const parsedRequest = parseResult.data;
  const taskIdString = parsedRequest.taskId;

  logger.setDefaultTaskId?.(taskIdString);
  logger.info("Processing task", {
    task_id: taskIdString,
    mode: parsedRequest.mode,
    filename: parsedRequest.filename,
  });

  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";

  // 3) Security check: Validate storage path for orchestrator references
  if (parsedRequest.requiresOrchestratorCheck && parsedRequest.storagePath) {
    const securityResult = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
      supabaseAdmin,
      taskIdString,
      parsedRequest.storagePath,
      parsedRequest.storagePathTaskId
    );
    if (!securityResult.allowed) {
      logger.error("Storage path security check failed", { error: securityResult.error });
      await logger.flush();
      return completeTaskErrorResponse(
        securityResult.error || "Access denied",
        403,
        'storage_path_access_denied',
      );
    }
  }

  // 4) Authentication is handled by bootstrapEdgeHandler.
  const isServiceRole = auth!.isServiceRole;
  const callerId = auth!.userId;

  // 4b) Rate limit non-service-role callers (workers use service-role and are not rate limited)
  if (!isServiceRole && callerId) {
    const rateLimitDenied = await enforceRateLimit({
      supabaseAdmin,
      functionName: 'complete-task',
      userId: callerId,
      config: RATE_LIMITS.userAction,
      logger,
      logPrefix: '[COMPLETE-TASK]',
      responses: {
        serviceUnavailable: () => completeTaskErrorResponse("Rate limit service unavailable", 503, 'rate_limit_service_unavailable'),
      },
    });
    if (rateLimitDenied) return rateLimitDenied;
  }

  try {
    const completionFollowUpIssues: CompletionFollowUpIssue[] = [];

    // 5) Resolve actor policy once (ownership + task user resolution).
    const taskActor = await resolveTaskStorageActor({
      supabaseAdmin,
      taskId: taskIdString,
      auth: auth!,
      logPrefix: "[COMPLETE-TASK]",
    });
    if (!taskActor.ok) {
      logger.error("Task actor resolution failed", {
        task_id: taskIdString,
        error: taskActor.error,
        status_code: taskActor.statusCode,
      });
      await logger.flush();
      return completeTaskErrorResponse(
        taskActor.error,
        taskActor.statusCode,
        'task_actor_resolution_failed',
      );
    }

    const completionAuthContext = {
      isServiceRole: taskActor.value.isServiceRole,
      taskOwnerVerified: taskActor.value.taskOwnerVerified,
      actorId: taskActor.value.callerId,
    };

    // 6) MODE 4: Verify referenced file exists
    if (parsedRequest.storagePath) {
      const pathParts = parsedRequest.storagePath.split('/');
      const isMode3Format = pathParts.length >= 4 && pathParts[1] === 'tasks';

      if (!isMode3Format) {
        const fileCheck = await getStoragePublicUrl(supabaseAdmin, parsedRequest.storagePath);
        if (!fileCheck.exists) {
          return completeTaskErrorResponse(
            "Referenced file does not exist or is not accessible in storage",
            404,
            'storage_reference_not_found',
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 7) Determine user ID for storage path from shared actor policy.
    const userId = taskActor.value.taskUserId;

    // 8) Fetch task context once (used by validation, generation creation, orchestrator check)
    const taskContext = await fetchTaskContext(supabaseAdmin, taskIdString, logger);
    if (!taskContext) {
      logger.error("Failed to fetch task context", { task_id: taskIdString });
      await logger.flush();
      return completeTaskErrorResponse("Task not found", 404, 'task_not_found');
    }

    // 9) Handle storage operations
    const storageResult = await handleStorageOperations(supabaseAdmin, parsedRequest, userId, isServiceRole);
    const { publicUrl, objectPath, thumbnailUrl } = storageResult;

    // 10) Validate shot references and update params if needed
    try {
      let updatedParams = { ...taskContext.params };
      let needsParamsUpdate = false;

      // Validate and cleanup invalid shot_id references
      const shotValidation = await validateAndCleanupShotId(supabaseAdmin, updatedParams, taskContext.tool_type);
      if (shotValidation.needsUpdate) {
        needsParamsUpdate = true;
        updatedParams = shotValidation.updatedParams;
      }

      // Add thumbnail URL if available
      if (thumbnailUrl) {
        needsParamsUpdate = true;
        updatedParams = setThumbnailInParams(updatedParams, taskContext.task_type, thumbnailUrl);
      }

      if (needsParamsUpdate) {
        await supabaseAdmin.from("tasks").update({ params: updatedParams }).eq("id", taskIdString);
        
        // Keep in-memory context in sync so downstream steps (generation creation) use updated params
        taskContext.params = updatedParams;
      }
    } catch (validationError) {
      logger.warn("Validation follow-up failed; continuing completion flow", {
        task_id: taskIdString,
        error: validationError instanceof Error ? validationError.message : String(validationError),
      });
      completionFollowUpIssues.push({
        step: 'validation',
```

import { useState, useRef, useMemo, useLayoutEffect } from 'react';
import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
import { useProject } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
import { usePanes } from '@/shared/contexts/PanesContext';
import { useUserUIState } from '@/shared/hooks/useUserUIState';
import { usePublicLoras } from '@/shared/hooks/useResources';
import { useLoraManager } from '@/shared/hooks/useLoraManager';
import { useIsMobile } from '@/shared/hooks/mobile';
import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
import { useUpscale } from './useUpscale';
import { useEditSettingsPersistence } from './persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence';
import { extractDimensionsFromMedia } from '../utils/dimensions';

interface UseImageLightboxEnvironmentProps {
  media: GenerationRow;
  shotId?: string;
  tasksPaneOpen?: boolean;
  tasksPaneWidth?: number;
}

export function useImageLightboxEnvironment(props: UseImageLightboxEnvironmentProps) {
  const { media, shotId, tasksPaneOpen, tasksPaneWidth } = props;

  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
  const { project, selectedProjectId } = useProject();
  const projectAspectRatio = project?.aspectRatio;

  const { value: generationMethods } = useUserUIState('generationMethods', {
    onComputer: true,
    inCloud: true,
  });
  const isCloudMode = generationMethods.inCloud;
  const isLocalGeneration = generationMethods.onComputer && !generationMethods.inCloud;

  const {
    isTasksPaneOpen: tasksPaneOpenContext,
    tasksPaneWidth: tasksPaneWidthContext,
    isTasksPaneLocked,
  } = usePanes();

  const effectiveTasksPaneOpen = tasksPaneOpen ?? tasksPaneOpenContext;
  const effectiveTasksPaneWidth = tasksPaneWidth ?? tasksPaneWidthContext;

  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const imageContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const variantsSectionRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const [isDownloading, setIsDownloading] = useState(false);
  const [replaceImages, setReplaceImages] = useState(true);
  const [, setVariantParamsToLoad] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null);

  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
  const variantFetchGenerationId = media.parent_generation_id || actualGenerationId;

  const [imageDimensions, setImageDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(() => {
    return extractDimensionsFromMedia(media, true);
  });

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const dims = extractDimensionsFromMedia(media, true);
    if (dims) {
      setImageDimensions(dims);
    }
  }, [media]);

  const upscaleHook = useUpscale({ media, selectedProjectId, isVideo: false, shotId });

  const editSettingsPersistence = useEditSettingsPersistence({
    generationId: actualGenerationId,
    projectId: selectedProjectId,
    enabled: true,
  });

  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
  const editLoraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
    projectId: selectedProjectId || undefined,
    persistenceScope: 'none',
    enableProjectPersistence: false,
    disableAutoLoad: true,
  });

  const effectiveEditModeLoras = useMemo(() => {
    if (editLoraManager.selectedLoras.length > 0) {
      return editLoraManager.selectedLoras.map((lora) => ({
        url: lora.path,
        strength: lora.strength,
      }));
    }
    return editSettingsPersistence.editModeLoras;
  }, [editLoraManager.selectedLoras, editSettingsPersistence.editModeLoras]);

  return {
    isMobile,
    selectedProjectId,
    projectAspectRatio,
    isCloudMode,
    isLocalGeneration,
    isTasksPaneLocked,
    effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
    effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
```

import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { TooltipProvider } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
import { Toaster as Sonner } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
import {
  DndContext,
  closestCenter,
  KeyboardSensor,
  PointerSensor,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
  DragOverlay,
} from '@dnd-kit/core';
import { sortableKeyboardCoordinates } from '@dnd-kit/sortable';
import { useHandleExternalImageDrop, useAddImageToShot } from '@/shared/hooks/shots';
import { useShotCreation } from '@/shared/hooks/useShotCreation';
import { useShots } from '@/shared/contexts/ShotsContext';
import { LastAffectedShotContext } from '@/shared/contexts/LastAffectedShotContext';
import { AppRoutes } from '@/app/routes';
import { useProjectSelectionContext } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
import { AppProviders } from '@/app/providers/AppProviders';
import { useAppDndOverlay } from '@/app/hooks/useAppDndOverlay';
import { useAppExternalDrop } from '@/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop';
import { getNetworkStatusManager } from '@/shared/services/network/networkStatusManager';
import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';

const AppInternalContent: React.FC = () => {
  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
  const context = useContext(LastAffectedShotContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useLastAffectedShot must be used within a LastAffectedShotProvider');
  }
  const { setLastAffectedShotId } = context;
  const { shots: shotsFromHook } = useShots();
  const { createShot } = useShotCreation();
  const addImageToShotMutation = useAddImageToShot();
  const handleExternalImageDropMutation = useHandleExternalImageDrop();
  const { handleDragStart, handleDragCancel, finalizeDropAnimation, overlayContent } = useAppDndOverlay();

  const handleDragEnd = useAppExternalDrop({
    selectedProjectId,
    currentShotsCount: shotsFromHook?.length || 0,
    setLastAffectedShotId,
    createShot,
    addImageToShotMutation,
    handleExternalImageDropMutation,
    onDropHandled: finalizeDropAnimation,
  });

  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor, {
      activationConstraint: { distance: 8 },
    }),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, {
      coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates,
    })
  );

  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <DndContext
        sensors={sensors}
        collisionDetection={closestCenter}
        onDragStart={handleDragStart}
        onDragCancel={handleDragCancel}
        onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
      >
        <AppRoutes />
        <DragOverlay zIndex={10000} style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>{overlayContent}</DragOverlay>
        <Sonner />
      </DndContext>
    </TooltipProvider>
  );
};

function App() {
  React.useEffect(() => {
    try {
      getNetworkStatusManager();
    } catch (error) {
      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
        context: 'App.startup.networkStatusManager',
        showToast: false,
      });
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <AppProviders>
      <AppInternalContent />
    </AppProviders>
  );
}

export default App;
```

## Assessment of `chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02` Branch

This is a **large refactoring branch (63 commits, ~2.7M lines deleted, ~64k lines added)** focused on architectural reorganization, code cleanup, and test improvements. Here's my detailed assessment:

### POSITIVE FINDINGS

**1. Edge Function Refactoring (Strong Quality)**
- **Extract-to-helpers pattern**: Functions like `calculate-task-cost/index.ts` extracted calculation logic to `costHelpers.ts` (312 LOC file). This reduces index.ts from 357 to 39 lines while keeping related logic grouped.
- **Handler extraction**: `complete_task/index.ts` → `handler.ts` (369 LOC) is well-motivated. The new `handler.ts` maintains the full logic flow while index.ts becomes a minimal server entry point.
- **Test coverage added**: New test files (`auth.test.ts`, `edgeHandler.test.ts`, `autoTopupDomain.test.ts`, `taskActorPolicy.test.ts`) with proper test structure.
- **No apparent breakage**: Import paths updated consistently (e.g., `edgeHandler.ts` now uses `bootstrapEdgeHandler` helper consistently).

**2. Hook Reorganization (Medium Quality)**
- **Namespace consolidation**: Hooks being moved into subdirectories (`ui-image/`, `shots/`, `tasks/`, `timeline/`, `variants/`) creates better discoverability and logical grouping.
- **Test updates follow moves**: Mock paths updated to match new locations (`@/shared/hooks/ui-image/useProgressiveImage`).
- **Key utility extraction**: `useImageLightboxEnvironment` properly extracted from monolithic `ImageLightbox.tsx`, breaking down a large component's setup concerns.

**3. Type Consolidation (Strong)**
- **Deleted compat shims**: `src/types/generationAndShots.ts`, `generationMetadata.ts`, `generationRow.ts`, `shot.ts` were re-export shims pointing to `@/domains/generation/types`.
- **No broken imports**: Git grep confirms no files reference the deleted compat paths—all imports updated to use canonical `@/domains/generation/types`.
- **Cleaner import surface**: Removing barrel re-exports makes the actual dependency graph clearer.

**4. .desloppify/ Cleanup (Good)**
- **Working directory deleted**: All ~550+ `.desloppify/` analysis/calibration JSON files removed. These are bot-generated working artifacts and don't belong in the repo.

### CONCERNS & REGRESSIONS

**1. Deleted Files That Still Exist**
- **MediaLightbox.tsx barrel deleted but subcomponents still exist**:
  - File `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox.tsx` was a barrel exporting `MediaLightbox` subcomponent
  - Imports changed from `@/shared/components/MediaLightbox` to direct subdir imports (`@/shared/components/MediaLightbox/MediaLightbox`)
  - **Status**: No breaking imports found; the change is correctly applied everywhere.

**2. Comprehensive Component Deletion (TasksPane)**
- **Entire feature deleted**: `src/shared/components/TasksPane/` folder (20+ files) including hooks, test files, utils, and components.
- **What happened**: TasksPane functionality appears to have been moved to `src/features/tasks/` (new folder, not in diff detail).
- **Risk**: Cannot verify without reading the new location. The git status shows this as deleted, not moved, which suggests they may have deleted the old folder and created new code separately.

**3. Test Churn**
- **Deleted test files**: 
  - `useHydratedReferences.test.ts` (136 lines, comprehensive tests)
  - `useLightboxOpen.test.ts` (66 lines, observer-based tests)
  - `useExternalApiKeys.test.ts` (9 lines, compat test)
  - These appear deleted without replacement visible in diff
- **Concern**: If these are truly deleted and not moved, the test coverage regressed.

**4. Hook Consolidation Issues**
- **useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts changes**: Test was heavily rewritten from 74 to 150 lines. The new test adds queryClient fixtures and cache assertions, which is good, but the old tests are completely replaced—unclear if functional coverage is maintained.
- **`getSupabaseClient` vs `supabase`**: Multiple test mocks changed from mocking `supabase` to `getSupabaseClient()`. This is correct if the implementation changed, but the pattern shift across many files suggests either a widespread refactor or test-by-test fixes.

### QUALITY ASSESSMENT

**Code Architecture**: **7/10**
- Positive: Hooks extracted to logical namespaces, types consolidated, edge function responsibilities split clearly.
- Negative: Some deletions appear incomplete (test files with no obvious replacement), and the TasksPane reorganization opacity.

**Breaking Changes**: **8/10 (safe)**
- No import paths left dangling that I can find.
- Type compat shims deleted with all imports updated.
- Barrel file deleted but all consumers updated.
- **Risk**: TasksPane deletion is the biggest unknown—could have broken features if the new `features/tasks/` implementation isn't complete.

**Test Coverage**: **6/10 (degraded)**
- Added new tests in edge functions and helpers (positive).
- But deleted standalone test files without clear replacements (negative).
- `useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts` rewrite suggests functional testing still present, but can't verify without seeing the actual hook.

**Bot-Generated Churn?**: **Moderate signals**
- **For**: Consistency across many hook test mocks updated identically (pattern suggests automation).
- **Against**: Deletion decisions (TasksPane, specific tests) require judgment; tests rewritten rather than replaced wholesale.
- **Conclusion**: Likely a mix of automated refactoring + manual cleanup. Not pure bot output, but bot-assisted.

### SPECIFIC RED FLAGS

1. **TasksPane wholesale deletion**: 20+ files deleted. Is it truly moved to `features/tasks/` or was it partially recreated? Need to verify the new location is complete.
   
2. **Test file deletions without visible migration**:
   - `useHydratedReferences.test.ts` (136 lines)
   - `useLightboxOpen.test.ts` (66 lines)
   - If these weren't moved, test coverage decreased.

3. **MediaLightbox imports change from barrel to direct**: While verified to not break, this is a significant UX change for consumers. Barrel files provide a stable interface.

### VERDICT

**Overall: 6.5/10 (Legitimate refactoring with execution gaps)**

**Recommendation**: 
- **Before merge**: 
  1. Verify TasksPane logic is complete in `src/features/tasks/` 
  2. Confirm deleted test files were truly moved or are no longer needed
  3. Run full test suite to catch any integration issues the diffs don't reveal
  4. Spot-check a few hook reorganizations to confirm they still function correctly

- **The branch is NOT bot-generated churn**. It's a real architectural reorganization with legitimate goals:
  - Consolidating types
  - Extracting helpers from large functions  
  - Organizing hooks into semantic namespaces
  - Deleting working-directory artifacts

- **But execution has gaps**: Some deletions are incomplete or opaque (TasksPane), and test coverage changes aren't fully transparent.

**Risk level**: **Medium** — Likely to work, but requires verification of reorganized features (esp. TasksPane) and test coverage for deleted test files.

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..3873da7bd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/IncomingTaskItem.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+import React from 'react';
+import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
+import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
+import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
+import { truncateText } from '@/shared/lib/stringFormatting';
+import { IncomingTask, useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
+import { getTaskDisplayName } from '@/shared/lib/taskConfig';
+
+interface IncomingTaskItemProps {
+  task: IncomingTask;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A "filler" task item that appears in the task list while actual tasks
+ * are being created in the background. Matches the visual style of TaskItem
+ * but shows a loading state instead of real task data.
+ */
+const IncomingTaskItem: React.FC<IncomingTaskItemProps> = ({ task }) => {
+  const { cancelIncoming } = useIncomingTasks();
+
+  // Get display name for task type, with fallback
+  const displayTaskType = getTaskDisplayName(task.taskType) || 'Task';
+
+  const truncatedLabel = truncateText(task.label, 40);
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      className={cn(
+        "relative p-3 mb-2 bg-zinc-800/95 rounded-md shadow border transition-colors",
+        "border-blue-500/50 animate-pulse overflow-hidden"
+      )}
+    >
+      {/* Header row: task type + loading indicator */}
+      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1 gap-2 min-w-0">
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 min-w-0">
+          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-blue-400 flex-shrink-0" />
+          <span className="text-sm font-light text-zinc-200 truncate">
+            Creating {displayTaskType}...
+          </span>
+        </div>
+        {task.expectedCount && task.expectedCount > 1 && (
+          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400 flex-shrink-0">
+            {task.expectedCount} tasks
+          </span>
+        )}
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Label/prompt display */}
+      <div className="mt-2">
+        <div className="bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-400/20 rounded px-2 py-1.5">
+          <div className="text-xs text-zinc-300 italic truncate">
+            "{truncatedLabel}"
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Footer: status + cancel */}
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">
+        <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">Preparing...</span>
+        <Button
+          variant="ghost"
+          size="sm"
+          onClick={() => cancelIncoming(task.id)}
+          className="px-2 py-0.5 text-red-400 hover:bg-red-900/20 hover:text-red-300"
+        >
+          Cancel
+        </Button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+
+export default IncomingTaskItem;

   415→            image={image}
   416→            localStarred={localStarred}
   417→            isTogglingStar={isTogglingStar}
   418→            isDeleting={isCurrentDeleting}
   419→            showStar={showStar}
   420→            showEdit={showEdit}
   421→            showDelete={showDelete}
   422→            onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
   423→            setIsTogglingStar={setIsTogglingStar}
   424→            setLocalStarred={setLocalStarred}
   425→            onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   426→            onDelete={onDelete}
   427→          />
   428→      </>)
   429→      }
   430→    </div>
   431→  );
   432→  return isMobile ? (
   433→    <React.Fragment key={imageKey}>
   434→      {imageContent}
   435→      {onCreateShot && (
   436→        <CreateShotModal
   437→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   438→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   439→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   440→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   441→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   442→        />
   443→      )}
   444→    </React.Fragment>
   445→  ) : (
   446→    <DraggableImage key={`draggable-${imageKey}`} image={image} onDoubleClick={() => onOpenLightbox(image)}>
   447→      {imageContent}
   448→      {onCreateShot && (
   449→        <CreateShotModal
   450→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   451→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   452→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   453→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   454→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   455→        />
   456→      )}
   457→    </DraggableImage>
   458→  );
   459→};
   460→
```

     5→import type { ReferenceImage } from '@/shared/types/referenceImage';
     6→import { useProjectIdentityContext } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
     7→
     8→/**
     9→ * Hook to hydrate reference pointers with full data from resources table
    10→ * Converts lightweight ReferenceImage pointers to full HydratedReferenceImage objects
    11→ * Searches both user's own resources and public resources
    12→ */
    13→export const useHydratedReferences = (
    14→  referencePointers: ReferenceImage[] | undefined
    15→): {
    16→  hydratedReferences: HydratedReferenceImage[];
    17→  isLoading: boolean;
    18→  hasLegacyReferences: boolean;
    19→} => {
    20→  // Get current user ID from context for ownership check (avoids extra auth call)
    21→  const { userId: currentUserId } = useProjectIdentityContext();
    22→
    23→  // Extract resource IDs we need to fetch
    24→  const resourceIdsToFetch = useMemo(() => {
    25→    if (!referencePointers) return [];
    26→    return referencePointers
    27→      .map(p => p.resourceId)
    28→      .filter(id => !!id); // Only valid IDs
    29→  }, [referencePointers]);
    30→
    31→  // Use optimized fetch for specific resources
    32→  const specificResources = useSpecificResources(resourceIdsToFetch);
    33→
    34→  // Fallback to searching all resources if we're not using the optimized path yet
    35→  // or if we need to search public resources that might not be in the specific list (though specific list should handle both)
    36→  // keeping these hooks for now to ensure we don't break other parts, but relying on specificResources for data
    37→
    38→  const isLoading = specificResources.isLoading;
    39→
    40→  // Combine all available resources
    41→  const allResources = useMemo(() => {
    42→    return specificResources.data || [];
    43→  }, [specificResources.data]);
    44→
    45→  const result = useMemo(() => {
    46→    // ... existing logging ...
    47→
    48→    if (!referencePointers || referencePointers.length === 0) {
    49→      // ... existing empty return ...
    50→      return {
    51→        hydratedReferences: [],
    52→        isLoading: false, // Nothing to load if no pointers
    53→        hasLegacyReferences: false
    54→      };
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    // ... existing hydration logic ...
    58→
    59→    let hasLegacyReferences = false;
    60→
    61→    const hydrated: HydratedReferenceImage[] = referencePointers
    62→      .map(pointer => {
    63→        // Check if this is a legacy reference (has data inline)
    64→        const isLegacy = !pointer.resourceId && pointer.styleReferenceImage;
    65→
    66→        if (isLegacy) {
    67→          hasLegacyReferences = true;
    68→          // Return legacy data as-is for now (migration will handle)
    69→          return {
    70→            id: pointer.id,
    71→            resourceId: '', // Will be set during migration
    72→            name: pointer.name || 'Reference',
    73→            styleReferenceImage: pointer.styleReferenceImage || '',
    74→            styleReferenceImageOriginal: pointer.styleReferenceImageOriginal || '',
    75→            thumbnailUrl: pointer.thumbnailUrl || null,
    76→            styleReferenceStrength: pointer.styleReferenceStrength ?? 1.1,
    77→            subjectStrength: pointer.subjectStrength ?? 0.0,
    78→            subjectDescription: pointer.subjectDescription || '',
    79→            inThisScene: pointer.inThisScene ?? false,
    80→            inThisSceneStrength: pointer.inThisSceneStrength ?? 1.0,
    81→            referenceMode: pointer.referenceMode || 'style',
    82→            styleBoostTerms: pointer.styleBoostTerms || '',
    83→            createdAt: pointer.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
    84→            updatedAt: pointer.updatedAt || new Date().toISOString(),
    85→            isPublic: false, // Legacy references default to private
    86→            isOwner: true, // Legacy references are always owned by current user
    87→          } as HydratedReferenceImage;
    88→        }
    89→
    90→        // Find the resource for this pointer in ALL available resources
    91→        const resource = allResources.find(r => r.id === pointer.resourceId);
    92→
    93→        if (!resource) {
    94→          // Only warn if we're not loading anymore
    95→          return null;
    96→        }
    97→
    98→        const metadata = resource.metadata as StyleReferenceMetadata;
    99→
   100→        // Check ownership - compare resource user_id with current user
   101→        const resourceOwnerId = resource.userId || resource.user_id;
   102→        const isOwner = !!(currentUserId && resourceOwnerId === currentUserId);
   103→
   104→        // Hydrate: Resource provides image data, pointer provides usage settings
   105→        // Pointer settings override resource defaults (project-specific usage)
   106→        return {
   107→          id: pointer.id,
   108→          resourceId: resource.id,
   109→          // Immutable data from resource
   110→          name: metadata.name,
   111→          styleReferenceImage: metadata.styleReferenceImage,
   112→          styleReferenceImageOriginal: metadata.styleReferenceImageOriginal,
   113→          thumbnailUrl: metadata.thumbnailUrl || null,
   114→          // Use pointer.createdAt (when added to project) for sorting, fall back to resource.createdAt
   115→          createdAt: pointer.createdAt || resource.createdAt,
   116→          updatedAt: metadata.updatedAt,
   117→          // Visibility and ownership settings
   118→          isPublic: metadata.is_public ?? false,
   119→          isOwner: isOwner,
   120→          // Project-specific usage settings from pointer (with resource defaults as fallback)
   121→          referenceMode: pointer.referenceMode ?? metadata.referenceMode ?? 'style',
   122→          styleReferenceStrength: pointer.styleReferenceStrength ?? metadata.styleReferenceStrength ?? 1.1,
   123→          subjectStrength: pointer.subjectStrength ?? metadata.subjectStrength ?? 0.0,
   124→          subjectDescription: pointer.subjectDescription ?? metadata.subjectDescription ?? '',
   125→          inThisScene: pointer.inThisScene ?? metadata.inThisScene ?? false,
   126→          inThisSceneStrength: pointer.inThisSceneStrength ?? metadata.inThisSceneStrength ?? 1.0,
   127→          styleBoostTerms: pointer.styleBoostTerms ?? metadata.styleBoostTerms ?? '',
   128→        } as HydratedReferenceImage;
   129→      })
   130→      .filter((ref): ref is HydratedReferenceImage => ref !== null);
   131→
   132→    return {
   133→      hydratedReferences: hydrated,
   134→      isLoading,
   135→      hasLegacyReferences
   136→    };
   137→  }, [referencePointers, allResources, isLoading, currentUserId]);
   138→
   139→  return result;
   140→};

import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useLightboxOpen } from '../useLightboxOpen';

describe('useLightboxOpen', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('lightbox-open');
  });

  it('returns false when lightbox-open class is absent', () => {
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('lightbox-open');
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    expect(result.current).toBe(false);
  });

  it('returns true when lightbox-open class is present on init', () => {
    document.documentElement.classList.add('lightbox-open');
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reacts when lightbox-open class is added', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    expect(result.current).toBe(false);

    await act(async () => {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('lightbox-open');
      // MutationObserver fires asynchronously
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    });
    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reacts when lightbox-open class is removed', async () => {
    document.documentElement.classList.add('lightbox-open');
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    expect(result.current).toBe(true);

    await act(async () => {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('lightbox-open');
```

1→import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
     2→
     3→type Listener = () => void;
     4→
     5→let openCount = 0;
     6→const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
     7→
     8→function notifyListeners(): void {
     9→  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
    10→}
    11→
    12→function getSnapshot(): boolean {
    13→  return openCount > 0;
    14→}
    15→
    16→function subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    17→  listeners.add(listener);
    18→  return () => {
    19→    listeners.delete(listener);
    20→  };
    21→}
    22→
    23→export function acquireLightboxOpenState(): () => void {
    24→  openCount += 1;
    25→  notifyListeners();
    26→
    27→  let released = false;
    28→  return () => {
    29→    if (released) {
    30→      return;
    31→    }
    32→    released = true;
    33→    openCount = Math.max(0, openCount - 1);
    34→    notifyListeners();
    35→  };
    36→}
    37→
    38→export function useLightboxOpenState(): boolean {
    39→  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
    40→}
    41→
    42→function __resetLightboxOpenStateForTests(): void {
    43→  openCount = 0;
    44→  notifyListeners();
    45→}
    46→
```

```
1→# Triage Tooling Fixes
     2→
     3→Fixes to the desloppify triage flow identified by 5-agent audit. Ranked by impact.
     4→
     5→## Master Checklist
     6→
     7→- [ ] **1. Path validation in ENRICH gate** (high impact, ~1 day)
     8→- [ ] **2. Effort tag requirement in ENRICH** (medium impact, ~0.5 day)
     9→- [ ] **3. Cross-cluster file overlap warning in ORGANIZE** (medium impact, ~1 day)
    10→- [ ] **4. Step title length cap** (low impact, ~0.5 day)
    11→- [ ] **5. Cluster dependency field support** (medium impact, ~0.5 day)
    12→
    13→---
    14→
    15→## 1. Path validation in ENRICH gate
    16→
    17→**The single most impactful change.** Would have caught 3 of 7 documented failure types.
    18→
    19→### Problem
    20→`_shallow_steps()` in `_stage_validation.py:232-247` checks that steps have a `detail` field but never validates that file paths in the detail actually exist on disk. Steps referencing `src/nonexistent/path.ts` pass identically to correct paths.
    21→
    22→### Fix
    23→
    24→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/_stage_validation.py`
    25→
    26→Add a new function `_steps_with_bad_paths()` that:
    27→1. Iterates all manual cluster steps (same loop shape as `_shallow_steps`)
    28→2. Extracts file paths from `detail` text using regex: `re.findall(r'(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+', detail)`
    29→3. For each extracted path, checks `Path(repo_root / path).exists()`
    30→4. Also tries common extension swaps: `.ts` ↔ `.tsx`, `.js` ↔ `.jsx`
    31→5. Returns `list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]` — `(cluster_name, step_index, bad_paths)`
    32→
    33→Call it from two places:
    34→- **`_cmd_stage_enrich()`** at line ~436 (after `_shallow_steps` check): if bad paths found, print warning with details but don't block (first pass is advisory)
    35→- **`_confirm_enrich()`** at line ~376 (after `_shallow_steps` re-check): if bad paths found, **block confirmation** with actionable error listing each bad path and which step it's in
    36→
    37→```python
    38→import re
    39→from pathlib import Path
    40→
    41→_PATH_RE = re.compile(r'(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+')
    42→_EXT_SWAPS = {'.ts': '.tsx', '.tsx': '.ts', '.js': '.jsx', '.jsx': '.js'}
    43→
    44→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
    45→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
    46→    results = []
    47→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    48→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    49→            continue
    50→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
    51→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
    52→                continue
    53→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
    54→            if not detail:
    55→                continue
    56→            bad = []
    57→            for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(detail):
    58→                p = repo_root / path_str
    59→                if p.exists():
    60→                    continue
    61→                # Try extension swap
    62→                alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(p.suffix)
    63→                if alt_ext and p.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists():
    64→                    continue
    65→                bad.append(path_str)
    66→            if bad:
    67→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
    68→    return results
    69→```
    70→
    71→**Repo root resolution:** Use the existing `command_runtime(args).root` or find `.desloppify/` parent.
    72→
    73→### Tests
    74→- Step with valid path → not flagged
    75→- Step with bad path → flagged
    76→- Step with `.ts` that exists as `.tsx` → not flagged (extension swap)
    77→- Step with no paths in detail → not flagged
    78→- Auto clusters skipped
    79→
    80→---
    81→
    82→## 2. Effort tag requirement in ENRICH
    83→
    84→### Problem
    85→Steps mix one-line fixes with multi-day refactors. No effort indication anywhere. A PM or executor can't estimate scope.
    86→
    87→### Fix
    88→
    89→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/_stage_validation.py`
    90→
    91→Add `_steps_without_effort()`:
    92→```python
    93→_VALID_EFFORTS = {"trivial", "small", "medium", "large"}
    94→
    95→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
    96→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
    97→    results = []
    98→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    99→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   100→            continue
   101→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   102→        if not steps:
   103→            continue
   104→        missing = sum(
   105→            1 for s in steps
   106→            if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   107→        )
   108→        if missing:
   109→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   110→    return results
   111→```
   112→
   113→Call from `_cmd_stage_enrich()` — **warning only** (not a blocker, since effort is a new field and we don't want to break existing workflows):
   114→```python
   115→untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   116→if untagged:
   117→    total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   118→    print(colorize(f"  Note: {total_missing} step(s) have no effort tag.", "yellow"))
   119→    print(colorize("  Consider: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small", "dim"))
   120→```
   121→
   122→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`
   123→
   124→Add `--effort` argument support to `_cmd_cluster_update()`:
   125→- New arg: `effort: str | None = getattr(args, "effort", None)`
   126→- In the `update_step` block (~line 678), add: `if effort: old["effort"] = effort`
   127→- In the `add_step` block (~line 661), add: `if effort: new_step["effort"] = effort`
   128→
   129→**File:** `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan_impl.py`
   130→
   131→Add `--effort` to the cluster update subparser with `choices=["trivial", "small", "medium", "large"]`.
   132→
   133→### Tests
   134→- Steps with effort field → not flagged
   135→- Steps without effort → flagged with count
   136→- `--effort` flag persists on step dict
   137→
   138→---
   139→
   140→## 3. Cross-cluster file overlap warning in ORGANIZE
   141→
   142→### Problem
   143→Clusters touch the same files without knowing it. The sequencing agent found 13 files referenced by 2+ clusters, causing potential merge conflicts and ordering hazards.
   144→
   145→### Fix
   146→
   147→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/_stage_validation.py`
   148→
   149→Add `_cluster_file_overlaps()`:
   150→```python
   151→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   152→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   153→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   154→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   155→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   156→            continue
   157→        paths = set()
   158→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   159→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   160→                paths.update(_PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]))
   161→        if paths:
   162→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   163→
   164→    overlaps = []
   165→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   166→    for i, a in enumerate(names):
   167→        for b in names[i + 1:]:
   168→            shared = cluster_files[a] & cluster_files[b]
   169→            if shared:
   170→                overlaps.append((a, b, sorted(shared)))
   171→    return overlaps
   172→```
   173→
   174→Call from `_confirm_organize()` in `confirmations.py` — **warning, not blocker**:
   175→```python
   176→overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
   177→if overlaps:
   178→    print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) reference the same files:", "yellow"))
   179→    for a, b, files in overlaps[:5]:
   180→        print(colorize(f"    {a} ↔ {b}: {len(files)} shared file(s)", "yellow"))
   181→    print(colorize("  Consider adding depends_on_clusters or sequencing notes.", "dim"))
   182→```
   183→
   184→### Tests
   185→- Two clusters referencing same file → overlap reported
   186→- Clusters with no file overlap → clean
   187→- Auto clusters excluded
   188→
   189→---
   190→
   191→## 4. Step title length cap
   192→
   193→### Problem
   194→Titles range from 64 to 271 characters. Long titles make the plan unreadable at a glance and unusable as commit messages.
   195→
   196→### Fix
   197→
   198→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`
   199→
   200→In `_cmd_cluster_update()`, when setting step title (~line 661 and 681):
   201→```python
   202→MAX_STEP_TITLE = 150
   203→if len(title) > MAX_STEP_TITLE:
   204→    print(colorize(
   205→        f"  Warning: step title is {len(title)} chars (recommended max {MAX_STEP_TITLE}).",
   206→        "yellow",
   207→    ))
   208→    print(colorize("  Move implementation detail to --detail instead.", "dim"))
   209→```
   210→
   211→Advisory only — don't block, just warn.
   212→
   213→### Tests
   214→- Long title → warning printed
   215→- Short title → no warning
   216→
   217→---
   218→
   219→## 5. Cluster dependency field support
   220→
   221→### Problem
   222→No way to express "do cluster A before cluster B." The sequencing agent found 8 cross-cluster dependencies that aren't documented anywhere in the plan data.
   223→
   224→### Fix
   225→
   226→**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`
   227→
   228→Add `--depends-on` argument to cluster update:
   229→```python
   230→depends_on: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "depends_on", None)
   231→if depends_on is not None:
   232→    # Validate cluster names exist
   233→    all_clusters = set(plan.get("clusters", {}).keys())

   237→        return
